# ML dataset log

## M3 interim products (derived, not downloads; `data/ml/interim/m3/`, manifest `m3_manifest.json` holds every SHA-256)

| Product | Content | Inputs |
|---|---|---|
| `<WINDOW>/static_factors.tif` | 43 float32 bands on the 5 km UTM grid (terrain, WorldCover shares, lithology shares, IMERG climatology, inside flag) | NASADEM, WorldCover, state geology, IMERG monthly |
| `<WINDOW>/labels_grid.tif` | event point counts and accuracy-circle weights (strict all years / in period) | GLC CSV |
| `<WINDOW>/events.parquet`, `events_all.parquet` | GLC rows in the windows with flags, radius, cell, prior-month rainfall | GLC CSV, IMERG |
| `<WINDOW>/cells.parquet`, `cells_all.parquet` | one row per inside cell (10,276) | all of the above |
| `<WINDOW>/imerg_monthly.npz` | 199 monthly series, native 0.1 deg | IMERG monthly |
| `lithology_mapping.json`, `grid_sensitivity.json`, `m3_manifest.json` | class mapping (no unmapped class), cell-size sensitivity, provenance | |
MISSING_FACTOR (not approved/downloaded): soil, NDVI, distance to rivers/faults/roads, SMAP, daily IMERG, SAR.
